# CSS — configuration

Complete selected records. The scientific account explains their scope; repeated passages retain exact values in the linked scientific records.

## C01

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C01</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/0</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C01</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C01</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Already decisive direct control or discriminator</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Does one available direct check or passive constraint completely settle this bounded task?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-34</p></li><li><p>ECSE-57</p></li><li><p>ECSE-61</p></li><li><p>P041</p></li><li><p>P042</p></li><li><p>ESS-003</p></li><li><p>ESS-046</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>P015</p></li><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>The same precise claim is already settled by valid, current, complete evidence or an effective passive constraint; no material stochastic, transport, delayed-effect, hidden-dependency or binding-duty question remains. Local reversibility is relevant but is not by itself sufficient.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Identify the exact state/claim and legitimate action; use the adequate direct evidence. A familiar indicator, atomic receipt/effect or simple invariant can collapse otherwise distinct states when its semantics actually establish them. Stop acquisition on this same settled claim; preserve residual different claims separately. If the bounded task is an actual lot or process-state decision, adequate current same-object evidence can remove repeated inspection or corrective verification. A genuinely complete, reliable, cheap exhaustive check may dominate a sampling plan. Completion of a count is insufficient when classification error is material; B02 preserves this distinction. R7: Adequate current evidence about the same lot, claim and time can remove new inspection acquisition. Adequate current effect or direct state evidence can remove a redundant effect check. A record does not create an alarm lifecycle, handoff or controller consumer where none exists. R8: The same-claim cheap path must match both actual state and action context. M03 accepts already adequate current evidence at zero new acquisition cost, and a direct current check can replace a larger history package. An earlier restored observation does not settle a changed current state; a lot census settles a lot claim, not automatically the process-effect claim. R10: The sufficient current discriminator remains an eligible complete route for its exact question. O01 distinguishes the accepted lot and even complete lot census from current process restoration; O04 distinguishes an effective current state from an implementation label. O06 shows why existing state evidence can remove new acquisition without answering a different dependency or learning question. R11: CW-A04 preserves the adequate existing state fact as a zero-extra-work answer to its narrow question. A bounded model is useful only if the actual question also needs action-context or dependency distinctions. Its availability imposes no new phase, diagram or review duty. R12 recovered integration: A direct current, valid observation may still settle the narrow state question without a work model or another acquisition. SC-A01 distinguishes that legitimate cheap route from stale, invalid or unknown feedback and from a different action-context, permission or gate question. A small model remains optional where it adds no material input; a missing action premise cannot be repaired by relabeling the same state fact.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>For a hypothetical local task, role A is the ordinary delegated task owner and has actual access to the object and controls. No extra approver is created by this study. Actual delegation, consequence bound and completeness remain to be supplied by a real application.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Existing evidence may have near-zero marginal acquisition cost. A new exact oracle can be expensive: compare acquisition cost, delay and remaining uncertainty under P041/P042 before buying it. No general deterministic-first spending rule. R7: Compare the real acquisition and consumption work that remains after justified reuse. L01 makes two same-claim responses feasible using stipulated valid existing records; this is not permission to use wrong-lot evidence. R8: No new check is required when valid current evidence already resolves the actual premise. A legitimately adequate independent protection or stop can remove the immediate restart demand; one-off disposition with no controller consumer remains bounded. R10: O06 uses zero additional acquisition for an already settled state, a two-unit direct state check when absent, and separately priced dependency/learning alternatives. These are stipulated conditional comparisons, not empirical costs or universal preferences.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/74.md#r-74b5d046a0210db3bec679b923f749716b1145da72309d985aacd0fbf0e26266">Shared scientific record 74b5d046a021</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/68.md#r-68626feb964e5012d1ddae4158855a6b4d5bbab70096df7649d9972023d36fc5">Shared scientific record 68626feb964e</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D29</p></li><li><p>D31</p></li><li><p>D32</p></li><li><p>D33</p></li><li><p>D34</p></li><li><p>D67</p></li><li><p>D68</p></li><li><p>D304</p></li><li><p>D305</p></li><li><p>D306</p></li><li><p>D307</p></li><li><p>D314</p></li><li><p>D315</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D318</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li><li><p>D334</p></li><li><p>D335</p></li><li><p>D336</p></li><li><p>D337</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X02</p></li><li><p>X09</p></li><li><p>V08</p></li><li><p>V09</p></li><li><p>A04</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>L01</p></li><li><p>L02</p></li><li><p>L06</p></li><li><p>M01</p></li><li><p>M02</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O01</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li><li><p>O06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>A changed object, premise, horizon, dependent system, duty or previously unmodeled loss reopens only affected claims. ## C02

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C02</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/1</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C02</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C02</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Human-mediated alarm and response</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Can detection, routing and response achieve the required state within the relevant horizon?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>P006</p></li><li><p>P021</p></li><li><p>P022</p></li><li><p>P024</p></li><li><p>ECSE-18</p></li><li><p>ECSE-19</p></li><li><p>ECSE-20</p></li><li><p>ECSE-21</p></li><li><p>ESS-011</p></li><li><p>ESS-034</p></li><li><p>ESS-035</p></li><li><p>ESS-036</p></li><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>P020</p></li><li><p>P017</p></li><li><p>P019</p></li><li><p>P059</p></li><li><p>ECSE-23</p></li><li><p>ECSE-24</p></li><li><p>ECSE-38</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li><li><p>ECSE-14</p></li><li><p>ECSE-61</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ECSE-60</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>A specific hazardous or consequential condition has a detector and a human response whose action can still alter the outcome. Detector output, human work and controlled process refer to the same event/state.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Define the event, denominator, losses and latest useful intervention; estimate or bound detector error and delay; route to a competent reachable actor; represent state and obligations; perform the permitted response; verify resulting condition at the scope justified. Receipt, acknowledgement and resolution retain distinct meanings unless a proved special case collapses them. When the already identified response path includes real lot inspection or process correction, its prespecified risk, inspection error, required work and effect verification constrain whether the response reaches its required stable state. The sampling or process source need not generate the warning. Conversely the actual alert window, resource and state criterion constrain the plan credited as a response. R7: Within the existing same-event response guard, risk-valid lot inspection and chronological corrective response are actual work and evidence inputs to the continuing alarm lifecycle ECSE-19. Multiplicity, current response demand, work changes, unfinished corrective/effect obligations and persistence of the actual purpose must be represented across the lifecycle horizon. ECSE-19 independently returns that response multiplicity, purpose and horizon to the work credited from P026/P027. ECSE-60 independently asks whether a proposed sensitivity objective remains justified given actual response capacity, missed-event consequences and the legitimate event/loss question. Neither relation requires the statistical source to generate the warning or establish the complete alarm system. R8: Retain all r7 alarm-lifecycle and sensitivity inputs and their separate reverses. If the response uses the C05/C07 controlled restart path, its existing action/context and effect distinctions must be carried through to that consumer; lot acceptance cannot be used as achieved-effect confirmation. This cross-configuration condition does not create another controller or alarm for an isolated lot transaction. R10: The same-event alarm response retains the actual risk-valid inspection/corrective/effect work and the full continuing-purpose horizon. If that response really uses C07 recovery, the consuming explicit mechanism must represent lot meaning, gate reachability, task-capable authorized available actors, action completion and current effect separately. This cross-configuration dependency does not create a control model or an alarm in an isolated transaction. R11: The repaired bounded model does not automatically create an automated-error oversight comparison or a representation experiment. Retain the actual alarm purpose, complete response and native timing/skill assumptions. A modelled work constraint may supply a component when actually consumed; it does not demonstrate human benefit. R12 recovered integration: Only when this alarm response actually uses the C07/C08 work model do its response sequence, current feedback, responsibility and shared-resource facts enter the safety timing and containment questions. SC-A02 applies a separately declared14-unit useful window to the actual complete-feedback endpoints13,18,18,12,17; it does not estimate alarm rates or replace a response distribution. The recipient safety purpose independently fixes the necessary completion state and timing. An automatic independent protection or genuinely absent response duty remains an alternative.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Model actor A owns alarm triage/response and backup B has explicit takeover conditions; they need control access, competence and stop/degrade authority. In X01 one pool has60response-minutes per1000opportunities and each alarm costs2minutes. No real staffing, peak timing or queue distribution is known. Average demand fitting the pool is necessary in that model but not sufficient for deadlines.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Count true and false alarm handling, interruptions/resumption, investigation, missed-event loss, nuisance suppression, ongoing tuning and the safety cost of a false stop. Lower alert count can lose true positives. No common numeric utility or optimum is invented. R7: Count the complete actual work per response and the number of responses on the shared lifecycle window: reentry, risk-valid inspection or diagnosis, justified action, necessary effect verification, stabilization and legitimate maintenance. Avoid double counting a genuinely shared adequate observation, but do not silently share separate duties or omit required effect work. In L01 the minimum valid n9 plan takes11 per response; two responses need22 against20. In L03 the high-detection policy cannot be justified by count alone, and deleting required effect work creates a false feasible total. R8: Risk-valid repeated inspection and actual required effect work remain in the same lifecycle window. Direct adequate current evidence may remove duplicate acquisition. One purpose resolved does not retire remaining purposes or confer retirement authority. R10: Count actual response multiplicity, complete tasks, resource conflicts and necessary maintenance on the same horizon. O06 distinguishes two three-unit responses on one actor with a five-unit window, genuinely separate competent capacity, and a low-volume single response. Earlier L01 two responses22 versus20 and L05 valid sampling/census comparisons remain separate exact cases.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/c0.md#r-c075200e6617a1fcdfe40f52398395b3102a2e5ab1411a944bb07041955c4548">Shared scientific record c075200e6617</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/87.md#r-877ca6d31a4687e75c8ddcc5242aa76185b3f4523c6fbd6aa0408d84f1ba31d0">Shared scientific record 877ca6d31a46</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/2c.md#r-2c76ea53b2b212181c5aff66981d9855d555d1fecea1e1c59c30350a31a878c7">Shared scientific record 2c76ea53b2b2</a></p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X01</p></li><li><p>X02</p></li><li><p>X04</p></li><li><p>X05</p></li><li><p>Y01</p></li><li><p>Y02</p></li><li><p>Y04</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V04</p></li><li><p>V05</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>A02</p></li><li><p>A03</p></li><li><p>A07</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B03</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>L01</p></li><li><p>L02</p></li><li><p>L03</p></li><li><p>M01</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O02</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li><li><p>O06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>New event prevalence, detector noise, human workload, duty, response deadline, necessary-service loss or common cause. R7: Reopen changed response multiplicity, event/risk definition, work sequence, effect evidence, resource pool, deadline, recurrence or remaining alarm purpose. A change in one of these may affect lifecycle maintenance without changing the warning generator.</p></dd><dt>R2 affected reasoning</dt><dd><p>R2: An actual safety-response deadline and completion/state condition constrain when the ongoing task may be interrupted and resumed. A later deadline can permit deferral; impossible competing deadlines need legitimate prioritization or another adequate path. Any reactive detector/threshold change must distinguish useful correction from noise amplification. Predicted burden/detection performance retains its actual regime and horizon. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking.</p></dd><dt>R3 reassessment</dt><dd><p>R3: A forecast of response demand must use the real event/actor mixture and shared time window. Manual false alarms or tuning can interrupt an active goal and add resumption work. Z01-Z03 expose these specific partial inputs; a correct detector alone does not establish a timely complete response.</p></dd><dt>R3 rule The loss/stakeholder decision in reverse defines the consequential alert and feedback distinction, without choosing a statistical threshold or creating response capacity.</p></dd><dt>R5 remaining premises</dt><dd><p>A02 has nonzero false signals and a different repeated-use probability; A07 separates indicator meaning from prevalence-dependent evidential value. Neither establishes a cause, understanding, injury rate or action authority.</p></dd><dt>R5 cost and adequate exit</dt><dd><p>An authoritative known change can be handled directly; a no-forecast policy has no forecast duty, and no-change remains legitimate for routine variation. Count real investigation and interruption costs.</p></dd><dt>R5 case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A02</p></li><li><p>A03</p></li><li><p>A07</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R5 rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Prior r5 current fields</dt><dd><dl><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>P006</p></li><li><p>P021</p></li><li><p>P022</p></li><li><p>P024</p></li><li><p>ECSE-18</p></li><li><p>ECSE-19</p></li><li><p>ECSE-20</p></li><li><p>ECSE-21</p></li><li><p>ESS-011</p></li><li><p>ESS-034</p></li><li><p>ESS-035</p></li><li><p>ESS-036</p></li><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>P020</p></li><li><p>P017</p></li><li><p>P019</p></li><li><p>P059</p></li><li><p>ECSE-23</p></li><li><p>ECSE-24</p></li><li><p>ECSE-38</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Define the event, denominator, losses and latest useful intervention; estimate or bound detector error and delay; route to a competent reachable actor; represent state and obligations; perform the permitted response; verify resulting condition at the scope justified. Receipt, acknowledgement and resolution retain distinct meanings unless a proved special case collapses them.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p>X01 shows high-sensitivity policy workload217minutes vs33.8minutes for a selective policy, with0.5 vs3expected misses. R2: An actual safety-response deadline and completion/state condition constrain when the ongoing task may be interrupted and resumed. A later deadline can permit deferral; impossible competing deadlines need legitimate prioritization or another adequate path. Any reactive detector/threshold change must distinguish useful correction from noise amplification. Predicted burden/detection performance retains its actual regime and horizon. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking. Y01–Y03 are returning-author re-examinations of reviewer witnesses; Y04–Y06 are stipulated mixture, local-objective and quantitative-cue models. ## C03

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C03</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/2</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C03</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C03</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Sequential evidence inside a bounded adaptive control policy</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Can a valid evolving evidence claim support the particular adaptive decision in time?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>P037</p></li><li><p>P038</p></li><li><p>P049</p></li><li><p>P052</p></li><li><p>P055</p></li><li><p>ECSE-05</p></li><li><p>ECSE-14</p></li><li><p>ECSE-21</p></li><li><p>ECSE-49</p></li><li><p>ECSE-50</p></li><li><p>ESS-011</p></li><li><p>ESS-030</p></li><li><p>ESS-045</p></li><li><p>ESS-051</p></li><li><p>P015</p></li><li><p>P017</p></li><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>P020</p></li><li><p>P019</p></li><li><p>P059</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ESS-046</p></li><li><p>P061</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>The adaptive decision, target, measured process, allowable adaptation and control horizon are specified. Statistical assumptions and operation inside the authority/envelope are separately supported. ECSE-50 remains unresolved as a general workload-inferred transfer policy.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Name the estimand and observation history; retain the selected inference method’s process/filtration and stopping conditions; bind evaluator/target changes; establish what its result says about the instantaneous or future controlled state; fit interpretation, permission and actuation dependencies to the deadline. Restrict, hold or use an alternate proven response when the required correspondence or capacity is absent. Only where an actual adaptive-control path consumes inspection or corrective work, intersect its native evidence conditions with the complete dependency schedule. B01 is fixed-plan acceptance sampling, not an anytime or adaptive inference theorem; no stopping guarantee is transferred between them. R7: Where an actual role transition occurs during the already selected inspection or corrective work, ECSE-05 consumes the selected lot/plan, which units are already inspected and which valid duties remain. The receiving actor and transfer window independently constrain continuity and feasible completion. Corrective diagnosis completed, action implemented and effect still unobserved remain different pending states. R8: Preserve selected-unit and pending-work continuity at actual reassignment. A transfer record should distinguish command stage, previous observation and current effect where the same C05/C07 decision consumes them. This does not prove uptake, adaptive-policy validity or a human-workload construct. R10: The current native control repair reinforces the already specified adaptation path only when it consumes that particular gate, role, state or effect input. O02 explicitly distinguishes a real parallel gate from a shared-resource gate and a gate ordered after observation; O03 separates role transfer from a name or credential. The statistical theorem and its own filtration/stopping conditions remain unchanged. R11: The exact evidence theorem and adaptive control premises remain distinct from model selection. A selected bounded model can name actual prerequisites and time constraints, but it supplies neither time-uniform validity nor a lawful adaptive policy. No additional CWA phase is a precondition for an already used partial input. R12 recovered integration: The actual bounded model may expose a deadline, ordered gate, shared resource and stale feedback that an adaptive action relies upon. These are material inputs to relevant timing, state and premise facets without proving the entire adaptive controller. SC-A01/A02 keep evidence validity, current state, actual authority and complete-path time distinct. No sequential theorem, filtration condition or adaptive-policy law is changed; a direct state guard or fixed allocation remains eligible.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Model A can interpret evidence but does not acquire risk-acceptance rights from a confidence interval. Model B owns the envelope/exception decision and must have real authority and availability. Tasks may overlap only when dependencies and staffing permit. X02 gives14units for the real modeled critical path, not the incorrect16unit serial sum. X04 has one exclusive operator for three2minute tasks in a5minute window.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Time-uniform width and continued observation cost, changing-evaluator bridge, human resumption, maintained fallback/readiness and exposure during waiting. A statistically valid result can arrive too late; a timely result can address the wrong target. R7: Count remaining unique work and actual transfer/reentry effort. Nine task completions can represent only five distinct inspected units when the receiver repeats the wrong subset. A correctly preserved selected set can avoid redundant work without dropping necessary coverage. R8: Avoid repeating valid same-claim current observation; count required new observation and real transfer/resumption work when the relevant state or context can have changed. R10: Represent actual precedence and resource constraints, then omit only genuinely unnecessary duties or reuse adequate current evidence. A different real resource can alter feasibility; renaming an actor or assuming overlap cannot.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/d2.md#r-d204b8796f423f8ea7b569b67b9adb70ea05e20a5452988482a40117b29b8f07">Shared scientific record d204b8796f42</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/e5.md#r-e5e6f6a7a473f2f613cab2133391979c9b0c9fa39cba9aa5e1899562d01751d6">Shared scientific record e5e6f6a7a473</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/6d.md#r-6dcfbd4699f129d0624f0af9b536d0e026a4281f34070405679a81d8a2c0535f">Shared scientific record 6dcfbd4699f1</a></p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X02</p></li><li><p>X03</p></li><li><p>X04</p></li><li><p>X07</p></li><li><p>Y01</p></li><li><p>Y04</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V02</p></li><li><p>V05</p></li><li><p>V07</p></li><li><p>A04</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B03</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>L04</p></li><li><p>M01</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O02</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Changed target, filter, process assumptions, policy, human capability, envelope or control deadline; empirical realization of workload-inferred policy remains an open question. A stable process can still be unacceptable; a planned experiment, justified containment or authoritative deterministic correction remains legitimate. Regime-specific or time-dependent forecasts can support bounded claims without global stationarity. A natural control band is not itself the safety requirement. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise.</p></dd><dt>R3 reassessment</dt><dd><p>R3: Monitoring and prediction apply to the complete retained-function outcome and actual response window. <dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C04</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/3</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C04</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C04</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Joint human-automation performance and learning comparison</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Does the proposed pairing or learned expertise improve the relevant work outcome over feasible alternatives?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-01</p></li><li><p>ECSE-15</p></li><li><p>ECSE-41</p></li><li><p>ECSE-47</p></li><li><p>ECSE-49</p></li><li><p>ECSE-63</p></li><li><p>P001</p></li><li><p>P029</p></li><li><p>P030</p></li><li><p>P032</p></li><li><p>P033</p></li><li><p>P034</p></li><li><p>P036</p></li><li><p>P039</p></li><li><p>P049</p></li><li><p>ESS-016</p></li><li><p>ESS-017</p></li><li><p>ESS-045</p></li><li><p>P031</p></li><li><p>P037</p></li><li><p>P038</p></li><li><p>P035</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ECSE-44</p></li><li><p>ECSE-52</p></li><li><p>ECSE-57</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ESS-022</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ESS-053</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>A task-specific human, automation or combined design is under consideration; outcome meaning, relevant population, learning feedback, context and feasible comparison conditions are specified.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Compare human-alone, automation-alone, fixed allocation and candidate joint designs where material, using total outcome/cost rather than a chosen weak baseline. Define experimental unit and interaction contrasts; protect or explicitly model learning, order, carryover and evaluator changes. Test cue relevance and actual retained role readiness. Interpret trained confidence and subjective workload separately from decision accuracy and safety authority. When the human/automation comparison actually assigns inspection or corrective duties, keep their measurement error, work, feedback and stabilized outcome in the same complete comparison. P026 does not become a human construct or learning law; P027 does not establish training transfer. Description of automation levels alone still activates no comparative study. R7: Keep the existing guarded human-automation and learning comparisons. The new native consumer analysis sharpens which actual work, state and outcome a comparison must address: preserved selection and pending duties are different from task-count totals, and lot/action/effect states are different performance targets. R8: The present repair sharpens what a bounded lot/action/effect observation warrants in a named consumer. It supplies no new treatment comparison, human learning effect or algorithm-human superiority claim. All r7 joint-performance and learning comparisons retain their original population and measurement conditions. R10: Where an actual joint-performance comparison credits the C07 mechanism, its outcome must include the required current effect, allowed context, task capability and complete recovery. O03 stipulates capability/permission/availability facts separately; O02 and O04 discriminate completion measures. These constructions describe what a meaningful comparison must preserve, not an observed comparison or intervention effect. R11: The CWA correction leaves the separately bound lot/oversight interfaces of D334-D337 intact. The absence of an oversight comparison in the particular C07/C08 model is not a global negative for ECSE12. Actual field expertise, automation benefits and EID comparisons remain separate empirical obligations. R12 recovered integration: A work model can specify the current state, effect, task capability, permission and available resources that an actual intervention comparison must preserve. It does not supply measured human performance, automation superiority, training transfer or a new display experiment. Existing separate ECSE12 lot/oversight relations remain exact; absence of an oversight study in one model does not erase them. No physical thermal or schedule number is transferred to people.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Study designer owns measurement/assignment; operations/risk authority owns permissible trials and action constraints. Actual users need training, feedback opportunity, time and usable controls. The model assigns these roles; no named organization or professional qualification was observed.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Training/rehearsal and skill maintenance, data collection, coordination, degraded cases, evaluation/confirmation and lost productive time. Adding people can improve some dimensions and worsen time/cost/tails. No full-factorial mandate. R7: Count measured and unmeasured outcomes at their actual targets. Reducing recorded tasks by dropping required effect verification is not a demonstrated efficiency improvement; reducing genuine duplicate work may be. R8: Current adequate observations may avoid redundant acquisition; retained human, comparison, training and transfer costs require their own evidence. R10: Include actual acquisition, coordination, effect verification, role access and maintenance where the comparison depends on them. Compare a competent unbranded actor, direct evidence, fixed allocation and actual parallel capacity. Credential counts, first movement or fewer recorded tasks are not outcome-preserving substitutes.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/c9.md#r-c9a09d04a4682f14c9a1c8a397883cac00a6738cd1220034e03c922eec360c22">Shared scientific record c9a09d04a468</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/0e.md#r-0e07447c6d9e5c3711842dedafd1af2e28d5eb220408338b38d8b050ceb2435d">Shared scientific record 0e07447c6d9e</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/f5.md#r-f52d07103bea724ab8500da06651867525e606a477af402240bcb15d91637af2">Shared scientific record f52d07103bea</a></p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X03</p></li><li><p>X04</p></li><li><p>X10</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y05</p></li><li><p>V01</p></li><li><p>V02</p></li><li><p>V03</p></li><li><p>A01</p></li><li><p>A06</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A12</p></li><li><p>A13</p></li><li><p>A14</p></li><li><p>A15</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B03</p></li><li><p>B07</p></li><li><p>L04</p></li><li><p>L06</p></li><li><p>M02</p></li><li><p>O02</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Changed cues, population, automation policy, learning feedback, retained skill, study unit or comparison target. Multiple endpoints, variants, probes and repeated looks define specific selection/stopping burdens; a descriptive or already decisive narrow result does not require that machinery. A genuinely continuous local design can use a response surface, with the native cognitive question fixing the objective and confirmation context. A fitted proxy optimum is not work improvement. A discrete, descriptive or already settled design does not trigger this route. Y01–Y03 are returning-author re-examinations of reviewer witnesses; Y04–Y06 are stipulated mixture, local-objective and quantitative-cue models. No operational efficacy is inferred. Actual field expertise, automation benefits and EID comparisons remain separate empirical obligations.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/c9.md#r-c9a09d04a4682f14c9a1c8a397883cac00a6738cd1220034e03c922eec360c22">Shared scientific record c9a09d04a468</a></p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-01</p></li><li><p>ECSE-15</p></li><li><p>ECSE-41</p></li><li><p>ECSE-47</p></li><li><p>ECSE-49</p></li><li><p>ECSE-63</p></li><li><p>P001</p></li><li><p>P029</p></li><li><p>P030</p></li><li><p>P032</p></li><li><p>P033</p></li><li><p>P034</p></li><li><p>P036</p></li><li><p>P039</p></li><li><p>P049</p></li><li><p>ESS-016</p></li><li><p>ESS-017</p></li><li><p>ESS-045</p></li><li><p>P031</p></li><li><p>P037</p></li><li><p>P038</p></li><li><p>P035</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ECSE-44</p></li><li><p>ECSE-52</p></li><li><p>ECSE-57</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ESS-022</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ESS-053</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X03</p></li><li><p>X04</p></li><li><p>X10</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y05</p></li><li><p>V01</p></li><li><p>V02</p></li><li><p>V03</p></li><li><p>A01</p></li><li><p>A06</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A12</p></li><li><p>A13</p></li><li><p>A14</p></li><li><p>A15</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B03</p></li><li><p>B07</p></li><li><p>L04</p></li><li><p>L06</p></li><li><p>M02</p></li><li><p>O02</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Review status</dt><dd><p>PENDING_CURRENT_R11_FIDELITY_THEN_DISTINCT_ADVERSARIAL</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R12 reassessment</dt><dd><p>A work model can specify the current state, effect, task capability, permission and available resources that an actual intervention comparison must preserve. ## C05

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C05</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/4</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C05</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C05</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Change, supplier and temporary-state assurance</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Which current conclusions survive a change in system, evaluator, interface or temporary operating state?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/b4.md#r-b44b584cc9c1ed05ef788324e663e463684a64b4c7319ca9604fd85d9bcdf438">Shared scientific record b44b584cc9c1</a></p></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>An identified change can affect a represented claim, observation process, coordination boundary, supplied item or control obligation. Unchanged unrelated claims need no automatic repetition.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Identify old/current objects, assumptions and interfaces; determine the causal reach of the change; justify evidence reuse; use a bridge or adequate discriminator with relevant error/dependence accounted for to separate system and evaluator changes; transfer state, authority, pending work and expiry at handoff; verify affected integrated effects before making the corresponding claim. Supplier and temporary-state assurance must distinguish the sampled lot, its inspection mechanism and error, the linked process history, the corrective action and its relevant effect. If inspection or correction is already required on a warning-to-stable-state path, its burden and evidence meaning are actual partial inputs to recovery. A generic feedback task alone does not create such a path; the existing path does not require the source to create its warning. R7: For an actual change or supplier-state handoff, preserve the lot and prespecified plan, selected and uniquely inspected units, remaining valid work, bounded disposition claim, linked defect history, diagnosis, implemented action and pending/observed effect separately. ECSE-05 consumes these at real reassignment; ECSE-48 consumes them as stable actionable content for the selected receiver and next decision; ESS-010 consumes lot/action/effect distinctions only when an actual controller model uses them. Their reverse purposes independently specify required content, timing and state distinction. R8: The actual changed supplier/restart path already names a controller, an action, context and an effect-dependent next decision. P026 therefore supplies bounded lot-status semantics to ESS008 unsafe-control analysis and to ECSE10 command-to-world closure; ESS010 model-state input remains separately material. The reverse action/context and next-effect purposes independently specify the required state distinction and recency. Admit these partial inputs without claiming the lot proves the entire hazard/control model. R10: For an actual supplier/change path that passes its state into C07, retain the selected lot and acquisition origin, implemented action, effect age, current context, actual gate criterion and currently assigned capable role. The independent receiving purpose determines which distinctions the exchange must preserve. A change to one of these can withdraw its own warrant while leaving an unrelated lot-plan calculation or direct result intact. R11: Lot identity, inspected units and meaningful lot/process distinctions remain relevant to current assurance. CW01/CW02 qualify the already existing C08 model of those objects, not a newly asserted historical change study. A new supplier or goal reopens the exact model boundary; no extra analysis is required when the direct question is already settled. R12 recovered integration: Changing feedback validity/age, operating mode, a shared resource, a gate, assignment or the needed cooling function can change the model-supported safety claim while leaving unrelated lot calculations intact. Reassess only affected premises and transitions. SC-A01 fixes the displayed restored status while varying validity and permitted context; accurate current direct evidence remains reusable. The present cases are new constructed work, not an authenticated historical supplier change.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Supplier provides accurate change/evidence information within its actual access; integrator retains system claim responsibility; evaluator owner has authorized measurement-change scope; accepting owner must be competent and have decision rights. These roles can coincide where allowed. X07 is a2×2comparison model, not a requirement for four empirical cells in every case.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Supplier coordination/access, bridging/targeted re-assurance, interruption, temporary-state exposure, case maintenance and verification. Extra hold points can prolong a vulnerable state; urgency can also leave real regressions unresolved. R7: L04 distinguishes nine nominal tasks from nine unique preselected units and retains the remaining five units plus transfer and stabilization work. L06 shows why action completion cannot replace the effect distinction needed by an actual restart decision. One adequate shared current fact may be reused; merely duplicating labels creates no independent evidence. R8: M01 separates stale and current effect. M02 shows why even complete current lot contents need not answer process restoration. M03 compares only the actual unresolved premise: use available current evidence, acquire a small direct discriminator where adequate, and retain additional process/history work only for its actual independent purpose or duty. R10: Use a concise existing accurate exchange or atomic semantics when they already distinguish the needed states. Count a real transfer/access delay and necessary new evidence. Do not demand a standard bundle or an extra checker merely because the information has an actual consumer.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/7e.md#r-7e5a809b35ed4555a59de92b27791b73adfea92870c9edf649271b78f3788518">Shared scientific record 7e5a809b35ed</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/af.md#r-af25dc1327a07064ac3b4cb164d6af7effd03722ec8064c9ed92c2c04398b3b8">Shared scientific record af25dc1327a0</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/8c.md#r-8c3114cc0bb94bc4e02a8c4c83d322c9af081588597430e9f8567593ef547fdf">Shared scientific record 8c3114cc0bb9</a></p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X05</p></li><li><p>X07</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y04</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>V10</p></li><li><p>V11</p></li><li><p>A04</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A10</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>B06</p></li><li><p>L04</p></li><li><p>L06</p></li><li><p>M01</p></li><li><p>M02</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O01</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li><li><p>O05</p></li><li><p>O06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Undocumented supplier change, exposed dependency, expiry, altered population/reference or newly observed transition hazard. R7: Changed supplier/lot, selected plan or units, receiving actor/decision, unverified effect, temporary state, shared failure or authority boundary reopens the affected interface.</p></dd><dt>R2 affected reasoning</dt><dd><p>R2: Changed regime weights can invalidate an old aggregate forecast even without within-regime deterioration. If assurance uses a real empirical before/after or supplier comparison, consequential pre-treatment exposure differences and target populations must be retained. Direct checks of an unchanged or fully determined claim remain sufficient. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking.</p></dd><dt>R3 reassessment</dt><dd><p>R3: Current assumptions, supplier performance and temporary configurations can already be consumers of a forecast or monitoring policy before their serialized records change. Scope follows the real dependency, including the needed horizon, actual claim meaning and anomaly-response work.</p></dd><dt>R3 rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D01</p></li><li><p>D02</p></li><li><p>D09</p></li><li><p>D10</p></li><li><p>D13</p></li><li><p>D14</p></li><li><p>D19</p></li><li><p>D21</p></li><li><p>D22</p></li><li><p>D41</p></li><li><p>D42</p></li><li><p>D49</p></li><li><p>D50</p></li><li><p>D55</p></li><li><p>D56</p></li><li><p>D61</p></li><li><p>D62</p></li><li><p>D77</p></li><li><p>D78</p></li><li><p>D91</p></li><li><p>D92</p></li><li><p>D95</p></li><li><p>D96</p></li><li><p>D99</p></li><li><p>D100</p></li><li><p>D110</p></li><li><p>D111</p></li><li><p>D136</p></li><li><p>D137</p></li><li><p>D154</p></li><li><p>D155</p></li><li><p>D156</p></li><li><p>D157</p></li><li><p>D158</p></li><li><p>D159</p></li><li><p>D164</p></li><li><p>D165</p></li><li><p>D166</p></li><li><p>D167</p></li><li><p>D168</p></li><li><p>D169</p></li><li><p>D170</p></li><li><p>D171</p></li><li><p>D172</p></li><li><p>D173</p></li><li><p>D174</p></li><li><p>D175</p></li><li><p>D192</p></li><li><p>D193</p></li><li><p>D194</p></li><li><p>D195</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R3 A generic feedback task alone does not create such a path; the existing path does not require the source to create its warning.</p></dd><dt>R6 remaining premises</dt><dd><p>Changed lot identity, inspection error, defect provenance or corrective mechanism can invalidate reuse and alter the actual hold/restart path. Retain adequate current evidence, targeted re-assurance and a one-off no-future-process alternative.</p></dd><dt>R6 case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>B06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R6 rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D334</p></li><li><p>D335</p></li><li><p>D336</p></li><li><p>D337</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R6 conditional member additions</dt><dd><dl><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-14</p></li><li><p>ECSE-61</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ESS-041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Meaning</dt><dd><p>Participants only at their specified actual component interfaces; Direct checks of an unchanged or fully determined claim remain sufficient. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. Transmit whether the lot is disposed, the action implemented or the process effect verified. A new supplier or goal reopens the exact model boundary; no extra analysis is required when the direct question is already settled.</p></dd><dt>R11 case refs</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>R11 rule refs</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>R11 relation limit</dt><dd><p>Membership is an index of named conditional interfaces, never a clique or proof of whole-configuration effectiveness.</p></dd><dt>Prior r11 current fields r12</dt><dd><dl><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Identify old/current objects, assumptions and interfaces; determine the causal reach of the change; justify evidence reuse; use a bridge or adequate discriminator with relevant error/dependence accounted for to separate system and evaluator changes; transfer state, authority, pending work and expiry at handoff; verify affected integrated effects before making the corresponding claim. Supplier and temporary-state assurance must distinguish the sampled lot, its inspection mechanism and error, the linked process history, the corrective action and its relevant effect. If inspection or correction is already required on a warning-to-stable-state path, its burden and evidence meaning are actual partial inputs to recovery. A generic feedback task alone does not create such a path; the existing path does not require the source to create its warning. R7: For an actual change or supplier-state handoff, preserve the lot and prespecified plan, selected and uniquely inspected units, remaining valid work, bounded disposition claim, linked defect history, diagnosis, implemented action and pending/observed effect separately. ECSE-05 consumes these at real reassignment; ECSE-48 consumes them as stable actionable content for the selected receiver and next decision; ESS-010 consumes lot/action/effect distinctions only when an actual controller model uses them. Their reverse purposes independently specify required content, timing and state distinction. R8: The actual changed supplier/restart path already names a controller, an action, context and an effect-dependent next decision. P026 therefore supplies bounded lot-status semantics to ESS008 unsafe-control analysis and to ECSE10 command-to-world closure; ESS010 model-state input remains separately material. The reverse action/context and next-effect purposes independently specify the required state distinction and recency. Admit these partial inputs without claiming the lot proves the entire hazard/control model. R10: For an actual supplier/change path that passes its state into C07, retain the selected lot and acquisition origin, implemented action, effect age, current context, actual gate criterion and currently assigned capable role. The independent receiving purpose determines which distinctions the exchange must preserve. A change to one of these can withdraw its own warrant while leaving an unrelated lot-plan calculation or direct result intact. R11: Lot identity, inspected units and meaningful lot/process distinctions remain relevant to current assurance. CW01/CW02 qualify the already existing C08 model of those objects, not a newly asserted historical change study. A new supplier or goal reopens the exact model boundary; no extra analysis is required when the direct question is already settled.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/7e.md#r-7e5a809b35ed4555a59de92b27791b73adfea92870c9edf649271b78f3788518">Shared scientific record 7e5a809b35ed</a></p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/b4.md#r-b44b584cc9c1ed05ef788324e663e463684a64b4c7319ca9604fd85d9bcdf438">Shared scientific record b44b584cc9c1</a></p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X05</p></li><li><p>X07</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y04</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>V10</p></li><li><p>V11</p></li><li><p>A04</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A10</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>B06</p></li><li><p>L04</p></li><li><p>L06</p></li><li><p>M01</p></li><li><p>M02</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O01</p></li><li><p>O03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li><li><p>O05</p></li><li><p>O06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Review status</dt><dd><p>PENDING_CURRENT_R11_FIDELITY_THEN_DISTINCT_ADVERSARIAL</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R12 reassessment</dt><dd><p>Changing feedback validity/age, operating mode, a shared resource, a gate, assignment or the needed cooling function can change the model-supported safety claim while leaving unrelated lot calculations intact. The present cases are new constructed work, not an authenticated historical supplier change.</p></dd><dt>R12 case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CASE_RESULTS_R12.json#SC-A01</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R12 relation limit</dt><dd><p>Actual named interfaces only; membership is not a clique, empirical effectiveness or whole-predicate proof.</p></dd></dl></dd></dl>

## C06

<dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C06</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/5</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C06</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Incident learning, reporting and verified action</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>What does an incident or report trend show, and which response has a justified prospective contribution?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-32</p></li><li><p>ECSE-39</p></li><li><p>ECSE-40</p></li><li><p>ECSE-58</p></li><li><p>P013</p></li><li><p>P025</p></li><li><p>P029</p></li><li><p>P039</p></li><li><p>P041</p></li><li><p>P046</p></li><li><p>P047</p></li><li><p>P051</p></li><li><p>ESS-039</p></li><li><p>ESS-040</p></li><li><p>ESS-041</p></li><li><p>ESS-042</p></li><li><p>ESS-043</p></li><li><p>ESS-058</p></li><li><p>ESS-059</p></li><li><p>ESS-065</p></li><li><p>P031</p></li><li><p>ECSE-23</p></li><li><p>ECSE-24</p></li><li><p>ECSE-38</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ESS-066</p></li><li><p>ECSE-25</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li><li><p>ECSE-10</p></li><li><p>ECSE-61</p></li><li><p>ECSE-19</p></li><li><p>ECSE-18</p></li><li><p>ECSE-60</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>Observed incidents, near misses, reports or indicators are being used to investigate a mechanism, allocate action or credit improvement. Exposure, detection/reporting process and relevant authority are part of the claim.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Preserve contemporaneous work and constraints; distinguish the observation process from true event occurrence; compare live causal rivals; separate fair accountability from causal explanation; implement an owned action; observe whether its intended mechanism and relevant outcome hold. Temporary containment may be justified before the cause is identified. The retained P027 mechanism contributes chronological defect provenance and owned corrective work to an actual learning or finding-closure episode. Cause discrimination, implementation and effect remain different stages. B04 leaves two causal worlds compatible with the same accepted lot and action log; B06 shows why changing the transmitted claim changes what evidence is needed. R7: When an actual alarm-linked corrective process is part of incident follow-through, chronological defect/diagnosis/action/effect evidence constrains continuing maintenance and one purpose-persistence premise. ECSE-19 independently identifies the actual continuing purpose and horizon for which effect or unfinished work matters. A generic incident record does not by itself create that alarm link. R8: Retain P027 chronological correction/effect evidence and actual incident/alarm purposes. The new P026 controller-input repair is not an assertion that a lot record is an incident investigation, reporting trend, leading indicator or organizational attribution. Actual event and denominator mappings remain required. R10: The existing incident/follow-through mechanism consumes stage-specific observation, causal alternatives, action interpretation and actual effect. Its ESS066 use is subordinate to this explicit mechanism. The actual warning-to-action linkage, continuing alarm purpose, common information, communication grounding and error-discriminating checks have separately typed meanings. A completed action or shared display does not establish current recovery or remove a distinct learning/accountability duty. R11: A bounded mechanism model can distinguish action implementation from effect and continuing learning without proving a cause or whole intervention benefit. Current state may be answered directly while justified learning remains. No historical reporting or intervention episode is invented by this repair. R12 recovered integration: The same bounded model can distinguish implemented action, observed effect, pending learning and accountability. A current effect may settle the immediate state while a separate learning or preservation obligation remains. SC-A01 does not turn an action label into effect; SC-A03 tests an explicit competing-loss branch rather than identifying an incident cause. This repair adds neither a reporting denominator nor an organizational intervention estimate.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Investigators need access to adverse information and real work; reporting participants need legitimate confidentiality/accountability boundaries; corrective owner needs resources and actual ability to change the mechanism. No organizational reporting process was measured here.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Investigation delay, observation/reporting burden, incomplete ascertainment, action inflation, recurrence exposure and lost service; prospective learning/verification value can remain even if the realized action does not change. R7: Count only actual linked corrective and verification duties. Do not turn every incident into a continuing programme; do not drop a necessary follow-through duty merely because its ticket is marked complete. R8: Direct current state can settle a narrow control premise while investigation, learning, accountability or another alarm purpose legitimately continues. Completion of one immediate decision is not a universal stopping rule. R10: A direct current effect can suffice for the immediate state question while an independently justified causal inquiry or alarm purpose remains. O06 makes the different purposes explicit. Preserve cheap direct mechanism checks, adequate containment, no-change and retirement only after its actual purposes and obligations are resolved.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/b1.md#r-b1d127eee5cef5d98bd35f2c4651238ea567bf83b80c763f3a343e3fa4d4ca79">Shared scientific record b1d127eee5ce</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/e5.md#r-e55592da416e15d628bcce7f9a5aa910b8cc71d30042db8899393917abfc63c0">Shared scientific record e55592da416e</a></p></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D35</p></li><li><p>D36</p></li><li><p>D37</p></li><li><p>D38</p></li><li><p>D39</p></li><li><p>D40</p></li><li><p>D110</p></li><li><p>D111</p></li><li><p>D154</p></li><li><p>D155</p></li><li><p>D156</p></li><li><p>D157</p></li><li><p>D158</p></li><li><p>D159</p></li><li><p>D304</p></li><li><p>D305</p></li><li><p>D306</p></li><li><p>D307</p></li><li><p>D308</p></li><li><p>D309</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D320</p></li><li><p>D321</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li><li><p>D336</p></li><li><p>D337</p></li><li><p>D340</p></li><li><p>D341</p></li><li><p>D350</p></li><li><p>D351</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X07</p></li><li><p>X08</p></li><li><p>X09</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y05</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V04</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>V11</p></li><li><p>A05</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A09</p></li><li><p>A10</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li><li><p>B06</p></li><li><p>L02</p></li><li><p>M03</p></li><li><p>O04</p></li><li><p>O05</p></li><li><p>O06</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Changed exposure, detection/reporting incentive, recurrence, counterevidence, new actor/resource constraint or changed corrective mechanism. Temporary containment may be justified before the cause is identified.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p>X08 makes the zero-event bound conditional on complete/known capture; unknown reporting sensitivity defeats the naive rate claim. X09 rejects the retrospective unchanged-action test. No causal efficacy of a reporting or culture programme is estimated. R2: A claimed comparative corrective-action or indicator effect can be distorted by pre-existing site, exposure or case-mix differences. The actual causal-control question determines relevant strata and interactions; blocking alone neither identifies an observational causal effect nor licenses hazardous experimentation. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking. Y01–Y03 are returning-author re-examinations of reviewer witnesses; Y04–Y06 are stipulated mixture, local-objective and quantitative-cue models. No operational efficacy is inferred.</p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X07</p></li><li><p>X08</p></li><li><p>X09</p></li><li><p>Y03</p></li><li><p>Y05</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V04</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>V11</p></li><li><p>A05</p></li><li><p>A08</p></li><li><p>A09</p></li><li><p>A10</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D35</p></li><li><p>D36</p></li><li><p>D37</p></li><li><p>D38</p></li><li><p>D39</p></li><li><p>D40</p></li><li><p>D110</p></li><li><p>D111</p></li><li><p>D154</p></li><li><p>D155</p></li><li><p>D156</p></li><li><p>D157</p></li><li><p>D158</p></li><li><p>D159</p></li><li><p>D304</p></li><li><p>D305</p></li><li><p>D306</p></li><li><p>D307</p></li><li><p>D308</p></li><li><p>D309</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D320</p></li><li><p>D321</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li></ol></dd></dl></dd><dt>R6 reassessment</dt><dd><p>The retained P027 mechanism contributes chronological defect provenance and owned corrective work to an actual learning or finding-closure episode. change</dt><dd><p>No corrective redesign when evidence shows the current control is adequate, with any continuing monitoring or accountability role retained.</p></dd><dt>Cheap</dt><dd><p>A direct mechanism check or targeted counterexample may resolve the next decision without a sprawling causal narrative.</p></dd><dt>Substitute</dt><dd><p>Change architecture or resources instead of training the last actor when that is the actual causal control.</p></dd><dt>Omit</dt><dd><p>Omit a proxy metric or broad culture score with no demonstrated decision contribution.</p></dd><dt>Retire</dt><dd><p>Close or retire actions only with implementation/effectiveness disposition and credible latent use preserved.</p></dd><dt>Invalid</dt><dd><p>Zero reports, a completed recommendation, a human-error label or a higher survey score is not a demonstration of controlled loss.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Rule <dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C07</p></dd><dt>Source</dt><dd><dl><dt>Filename</dt><dd><p>CONFIGURATIONS.json</p></dd><dt>Sha256</dt><dd><p>dbe7de37819ba8dc7b333da99569d8cdb946cb0956fd2049da7ef5e4f0996a62</p></dd><dt>Pointer</dt><dd><p>/configurations/6</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Narrative sections</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CONFIGURATIONS</p></li><li><p>ALTERNATIVES</p></li><li><p>C07</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Id</dt><dd><p>C07</p></dd><dt>Name</dt><dd><p>Fallback, containment and recovery under shared failure</p></dd><dt>Question</dt><dd><p>Which degraded response controls the actual loss when primary or protective paths fail?</p></dd><dt>Members</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/af.md#r-afc4b41e469bb52993381b81279ea63a42ad0a051da59f24696c6f4996f9e35f">Shared scientific record afc4b41e469b</a></p></dd><dt>Guard</dt><dd><p>A specific primary failure, unsafe transition or shared dependency can defeat a credited protection; continuation, stop, isolation, degradation and restart have concrete competing loss paths.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Map common power/sensor/software/maintenance/information paths; distinguish physical redundancy from information independence; evaluate reachable transition states and needed service; confirm the achieved state with adequate evidence; restore compatible constraints and feedback before the corresponding restart acceptance. Inspect the actual fallback/restart dependencies: a required inspection or process correction can consume the recovery window or leave the restored-state claim unsupported. Risk-admissible sampling and causal/effect evidence remain necessary only when that path actually relies on them. The source supplies a component, not the complete fallback or hazard proof. R7: Where the actual fallback or restart controller consumes lot/action/effect state, ESS-010 needs those distinctions to support its next permitted action. P026 qualifies what a lot-status observation means; P027 supplies bounded effect feedback when a restoration claim is actually made. The actual control need independently specifies the necessary state distinction and recency. R8: At the already identified fallback/restart controller, the same lot observation is an actual partial input to ESS008 action/context/feedback analysis and ECSE10 command-effect closure. P026 qualifies the bounded lot claim; P027 remains a distinct source of chronological corrective and effect feedback. Accepted lot plus implemented action remains compatible with both restored and persistent-fault states. Reverse purposes independently demand the relevant current effect and context, without changing lot risks or granting restart authority. R9: The already identified lot observation can serve ESS010, ESS008 and ECSE10 while retaining one acquisition origin. P026 supplies the checked lot/selection/measurement correspondence to ESS013 informational-dependence analysis. ESS013 separately specifies the traceability and interpretation needed when this result is offered as independent evidence. Neither direction identifies the lot items as physical protection channels. P027 action/effect and its supported explicit-mechanism relations remain distinct; a process-effect aggregation link must be supplied before applying that separate evidence-lineage relation. R10: The explicit C07 recovery mechanism already consumes P026 lot-status semantics, P061 real gate/evidence dependencies and P062 task-specific role capability; these are actual partial inputs to ESS066’s stronger causal/control/feedback form. Native cognitive inputs identify the joint activity boundary, goals and means, current external state, allocation and residual work, reachable role transitions, pending commitments, source/currentness, command-to-effect closure, actual actor access, complete recovery, interruptions, shared capacity, recipient meaning, causal alternatives, fallback and whole-task performance. Each direction is separately justified in its native frame; ESS066’s actual action/state/time/dependency purpose independently constrains those inputs. No sampled lot is thereby mapped onto the separate physical channels. R11: The existing C07 route is actually represented in C08/T05. The selected bounded CWA analysis consumes lot/process distinctions, real gate prerequisites and task-specific role/resource facts; its checked output contributes to the explicit ESS066 action/context/dependency account. P061/P062 also constrain the already identified ECSE02 means-ends model itself. Each return is the independently fixed goal, task and useful window, not symmetry. CW-A02 computes completion13 with a real parallel gate resource,18 with shared capacity or a genuinely later prerequisite,17 when the nominal extra actor is unavailable until4, and12 when the gate is genuinely unnecessary. These are new finite author values, not replacement values for the old cases. R12 recovered integration: The already represented C07/C08 work model supplies actual timing, feedback, task responsibilities, shared resources, operating constraints and recovery conditions to the safety facets named in the41 native frames. SC-A01 leaves invalid/stale/unknown feedback unestablished and distinguishes actual permission/gate/context. SC-A02 evaluates final required feedback, not the earlier restoration endpoint, against a separately fixed14-unit window. SC-A03 adds a declared finite thermal branch: with residual heat2, initial95 and hazard100, removing cooling reaches101 at step3, while controlled cooling3 gives90 at step5; with residual heat0, a simple power cut holds95 and is adequate in the model. This is one process, not the separate X06 physical-channel architecture. A work model makes these premises assessable; it does not prove their real-world truth, grant authority or settle every safety predicate.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Protection controller and authorized operator have actual actuation capability; recovery owner has access, competence and current state; restart acceptance follows the applicable authority, with separate personnel only where required. X05usesone thermal model and X06usesone assumed probability model; no equipment is operated.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Maintained standby capability, practice, testing, source diversity, voter/arbitration faults, false trips, delay, service unavailability and restart work. A second channel can help under correlation and still fail a required independence claim. R7: Retain actual effect work when the next action requires it, but permit a complete current precondition to replace unnecessary historical reconstruction. Do not multiply observations that share one failure into independent protection. R8: M01 requires the actual context and sufficiently current effect; a maintenance context can preclude restart even after a restored observation. M03 permits valid current evidence, a small direct check or an independently adequate protected hold. Keep actual required work in the path; do not require a larger history campaign solely to close an already settled narrow state question. R9: The existing adequate trace may be sufficient with no new acquisition or worksheet. Several legitimate consumers may reuse one observation. A direct current discriminator, justified protective state or actually available complete inspection can answer its own question without multiplying evidence credit. R10: O02 enumerates real task precedence and exclusive resources: the baseline parallel gate path completes at13 against14, a shared-resource gate or post-observation gate at17, and a longer gate at15; a genuinely unnecessary gate can be omitted. O03 separately checks capability, task permission, role activation and availability at the needed start. Additional real duties may defeat the joint schedule; a genuinely different competent resource can restore feasibility. Adequate existing current effect, direct checking, valid census for its lot question, protected hold, simpler unbranded workflow and no diagram remain eligible.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and Forecast recovery capability is conditional on the credited regime and mission. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking.</p></dd><dt>R3 reassessment</dt><dd><p>R3: Actual recovery credit includes the whole path and its resource/cue regime. additions</dt><dd><dl><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ESS-066</p></li><li><p>P061</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ECSE-52</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Meaning</dt><dd><p>Optional native component participants under the stated actual-use guards; this member list is not an unconditional task or procedure prescription.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R5 reassessment</dt><dd><p>The retained explicit barrier mechanism consumes actual joint-state/dependence and complete response facts. Real gates and demonstrated roles on the recovery path affect feasibility; labels and nominal staffing do not. Physical lifetime evidence remains separate from the human retention or recognition transfer law. If this actual automated barrier is described with levels, its task-specific failure/recovery evaluation consumes the named safety constraint, barrier state, dependence and restored-state criteria. A15 does not turn feasibility into acceptance. Current users and feedback interpretation can separately reopen ESS-001; the added endpoint participants are conditional, not new mandatory analysis stages.</p></dd><dt>R5 remaining premises</dt><dd><p>A09 supplies alternative joint distributions with fixed marginals, not empirical independence. A11 is a stipulated critical path, not an observed capable human. A12 leaves the actual human-transfer unknowns unresolved.</p></dd><dt>R5 cost and adequate exit</dt><dd><p>A credible passive bound, safe stop, direct known barrier state, genuinely available independent resource or removal of an unnecessary role can change the configuration without arbitrary extra testing.</p></dd><dt>R5 case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>A09</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A12</p></li><li><p>A15</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R5 rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D308</p></li><li><p>D309</p></li><li><p>D312</p></li><li><p>D313</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D320</p></li><li><p>D321</p></li><li><p>D326</p></li><li><p>D327</p></li><li><p>D328</p></li><li><p>D329</p></li><li><p>D330</p></li><li><p>D331</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Prior r5 current fields</dt><dd><dl><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>ECSE-10</p></li><li><p>ECSE-14</p></li><li><p>ECSE-25</p></li><li><p>ECSE-37</p></li><li><p>ECSE-38</p></li><li><p>P016</p></li><li><p>P044</p></li><li><p>P045</p></li><li><p>P047</p></li><li><p>P048</p></li><li><p>ESS-013</p></li><li><p>ESS-014</p></li><li><p>ESS-023</p></li><li><p>ESS-033</p></li><li><p>ESS-035</p></li><li><p>ESS-036</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ESS-050</p></li><li><p>ESS-053</p></li><li><p>ECSE-20</p></li><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>P020</p></li><li><p>P017</p></li><li><p>P019</p></li><li><p>P059</p></li><li><p>ECSE-23</p></li><li><p>ECSE-24</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>ESS-066</p></li><li><p>P061</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>ECSE-52</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>Map common power/sensor/software/maintenance/information paths; distinguish physical redundancy from information independence; evaluate reachable transition states and needed service; confirm the achieved state with adequate evidence; restore compatible constraints and feedback before the corresponding restart acceptance.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p>X05shutdown removes both cooling and feedback and can worsen the modeled state. X06correlated dualfailure.0298improves on.118single but exceeds.013924naiveproduct; adding.1voterfailure produces.12682worse than single. Neither probability is an observed plant rate. R2: An emergency or restart path can displace active work and must account for its actual resumption/state conditions. A noise-driven mode switch or repeatedly retuned restart policy is an intervention whose effect needs a discriminator; known direct corrections and justified containment are preserved. Forecast recovery capability is conditional on the credited regime and mission. The exact quantitative cue must distinguish the required state from a natural band or aggregate capability summary; charting is justified only for its actual consumer. Direct adequate state evidence remains a valid alternative. Forecast-based constraint assurance retains its mission/regime premise. When an actual process result is transferred or selected as a cross-check, its limit/regime/claim meaning is part of what recipients must understand or what error the check can detect. Correct communication does not make an invalid model true, and a correct model does not prove uptake or independent checking. Y01–Y03 are returning-author re-examinations of reviewer witnesses; Y04–Y06 are stipulated mixture, local-objective and quantitative-cue models. No operational efficacy is inferred.</p></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>X02</p></li><li><p>X05</p></li><li><p>X06</p></li><li><p>X08</p></li><li><p>Y01</p></li><li><p>Y02</p></li><li><p>Y04</p></li><li><p>Y06</p></li><li><p>V02</p></li><li><p>V05</p></li><li><p>V06</p></li><li><p>V07</p></li><li><p>A09</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A12</p></li><li><p>A15</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D06</p></li><li><p>D09</p></li><li><p>D10</p></li><li><p>D13</p></li><li><p>D14</p></li><li><p>D15</p></li><li><p>D16</p></li><li><p>D17</p></li><li><p>D18</p></li><li><p>D45</p></li><li><p>D46</p></li><li><p>D67</p></li><li><p>D68</p></li><li><p>D69</p></li><li><p>D70</p></li><li><p>D85</p></li><li><p>D86</p></li><li><p>D91</p></li><li><p>D92</p></li><li><p>D103</p></li><li><p>D104</p></li><li><p>D105</p></li><li><p>D130</p></li><li><p>D131</p></li><li><p>D132</p></li><li><p>D133</p></li><li><p>D134</p></li><li><p>D135</p></li><li><p>D136</p></li><li><p>D137</p></li><li><p>D154</p></li><li><p>D155</p></li><li><p>D156</p></li><li><p>D157</p></li><li><p>D158</p></li><li><p>D159</p></li><li><p>D200</p></li><li><p>D201</p></li><li><p>D304</p></li><li><p>D305</p></li><li><p>D306</p></li><li><p>D307</p></li><li><p>D308</p></li><li><p>D309</p></li><li><p>D312</p></li><li><p>D313</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D320</p></li><li><p>D321</p></li><li><p>D326</p></li><li><p>D327</p></li><li><p>D328</p></li><li><p>D329</p></li><li><p>D330</p></li><li><p>D331</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li></ol></dd></dl></dd><dt>R6 reassessment</dt><dd><p>Inspect the actual fallback/restart dependencies: a required inspection or process correction can consume the recovery window or leave the restored-state claim unsupported.
