Risk-admissible sampling and causal/effect evidence remain necessary only when that path actually relies on them. The source supplies a component, not the complete fallback or hazard proof. R7: Where the actual fallback or restart controller consumes lot/action/effect state, ESS-010 needs those distinctions to support its next permitted action. P026 qualifies what a lot-status observation means; P027 supplies bounded effect feedback when a restoration claim is actually made. The actual control need independently specifies the necessary state distinction and recency. R8: At the already identified fallback/restart controller, the same lot observation is an actual partial input to ESS008 action/context/feedback analysis and ECSE10 command-effect closure. P026 qualifies the bounded lot claim; P027 remains a distinct source of chronological corrective and effect feedback. Accepted lot plus implemented action remains compatible with both restored and persistent-fault states. Reverse purposes independently demand the relevant current effect and context, without changing lot risks or granting restart authority. R9: The already identified lot observation can serve ESS010, ESS008 and ECSE10 while retaining one acquisition origin. P026 supplies the checked lot/selection/measurement correspondence to ESS013 informational-dependence analysis. ESS013 separately specifies the traceability and interpretation needed when this result is offered as independent evidence. Neither direction identifies the lot items as physical protection channels. P027 action/effect and its supported explicit-mechanism relations remain distinct; a process-effect aggregation link must be supplied before applying that separate evidence-lineage relation. R10: The explicit C07 recovery mechanism already consumes P026 lot-status semantics, P061 real gate/evidence dependencies and P062 task-specific role capability; these are actual partial inputs to ESS066’s stronger causal/control/feedback form. Native cognitive inputs identify the joint activity boundary, goals and means, current external state, allocation and residual work, reachable role transitions, pending commitments, source/currentness, command-to-effect closure, actual actor access, complete recovery, interruptions, shared capacity, recipient meaning, causal alternatives, fallback and whole-task performance. Each direction is separately justified in its native frame; ESS066’s actual action/state/time/dependency purpose independently constrains those inputs. No sampled lot is thereby mapped onto the separate physical channels.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/83.md#r-83d2be5c3bdc985dcc6ea2a42f28c6a51534409cc19e6567a5b25ab8da85da25">Shared scientific record 83d2be5c3bdc</a></p></dd><dt>Rule SC-A02 evaluates final required feedback, not the earlier restoration endpoint, against a separately fixed14-unit window. SC-A03 adds a declared finite thermal branch: with residual heat2, initial95 and hazard100, removing cooling reaches101 at step3, while controlled cooling3 gives90 at step5; with residual heat0, a simple power cut holds95 and is adequate in the model. This is one process, not the separate X06 physical-channel architecture. A work model makes these premises assessable; it does not prove their real-world truth, grant authority or settle every safety predicate.</p></dd><dt>R12 case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>CASE_RESULTS_R12.json#SC-A01</p></li><li><p>CASE_RESULTS_R12.json#SC-A02</p></li><li><p>CASE_RESULTS_R12.json#SC-A03</p></li></ol></dd><dt>R12 relation limit</dt><dd><p>Actual named interfaces only; membership is not a clique, empirical effectiveness or whole-predicate proof.</p></dd></dl></dd></dl>

A mere menu of available methods does not activate all of them.</p></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><p>State the live and credible latent consumer; compare feasible portfolios and their residual coverage; account for common evidence and shared resources; satisfy actual binding constraints; choose or retain the smallest adequate combination; review retirement after changes. When utilities are unavailable use transparent qualitative dominance or bounds rather than invented precision. When inspection or corrective evidence has an actual or credible latent consumer, compare its real sampling, classification, diagnostic, effect-check, communication and maintenance burden with adequate alternatives. Its value is bounded by the claim it discriminates, not by the existence of a completed record. R7: In the actual choice among work/evidence alternatives, a small checked means–ends/resource model ECSE-02 can consume P026 risk-valid lot-disposition means and P027 future-quality correction/effect dependencies. Its reverse purpose distinguishes which goal and constraints the evidence must address. Actual continuing alarm maintenance and sensitivity questions can separately consume the same work/effect facts through ECSE-19/60. This is a set of named consumers with independent guards, not a clique or a requirement to use a full CWA or special display. R8: A small actual ECSE02 work-domain model is already present and remains material. The bounded negatives for ECSE26/45/46 now name the additional display, selected CWA phase or interface-comparison consumer that remains absent. The controller/action/effect repair is also a direct native comparison, not a clique from sharing a model. R9: The portfolio compares the information supplied by actual acquisitions separately from the number of downstream uses. The C07/H04 source-to-consumer correspondence is an actual input when this portfolio credits the same lot observation. ESS013 conditionally joins that informational account; its presence does not add a physical redundancy architecture or make the member list a clique. The receiving independence question constrains how the P026 record may be reused, not its fixed-plan likelihood. R10: The portfolio’s actual small means–ends model and controlled recovery question distinguish lot disposition, current effect, dependency understanding, learning and continuing protection. Native ECSE02/19/21/28/34/35/38/41/60 inputs condition its ESS066 mechanism according to those real purposes; neither an optional CWA label nor a diagram establishes another display, phase or intervention. Root r9 acquisition-origin and independent reuse purposes remain part of the actual information account. R11: The existing small checked means-ends/resource model is itself a permitted output of ECSE45. No additional phase, separate design question, complete programme or empirical efficacy is needed to acknowledge its actual partial input. CW-A01 separately computes sampling risk and total work before the model selects a route; CW-A04 chooses the needed portfolio for state, structure and learning questions. A direct state fact cannot answer a structural-dependency question, while a needless model can be omitted for state-only use. P026/P027 supply different native functions; P061/P062 supply actual evidence and resource constraints. The counterpart purpose constrains each input independently. R12 recovered integration: ECSE02 and ECSE45 legitimately share the already actual small means–ends/task/resource model. Its bounded outputs materially inform the32 r11-reviewed safety directions and the independently examined neighboring facets without requiring a further CWA phase. They remain one model and one source of information, not two independent corroborations. The independently fixed receiver purpose constrains model scope, fidelity and useful timing in return. Existing state-only, small-sketch, actual parallel-capacity, adequate passive-protection and genuinely no-new-artifact routes remain. SC-A03 explicitly retains simple stop when it is sufficient; its failing active-heat branch does not create a universal preference for continued operation.</p></dd><dt>Authority and capacity</dt><dd><p>Named decision owner controls budget and work, while any risk acceptor has legitimate remit; independent challenge has real information/access where warranted. Author does not grant waiver rights. Actual staff, permissions, response window and normative obligations remain application inputs.</p></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Acquisition plus interpretation, coordination, resumption, training, maintenance, false alarms, delay/exposure, duplicated review and evidence preservation. Costs share resources and cannot always be summed as independent project totals. Categorical duties constrain the feasible set before economic comparison. R7: Compare actual acquisition, use, maintenance, delay and missed-event consequences over the correct horizon. L05 distinguishes a fast but risk-invalid n8 plan, a valid n9 plan that misses the joint window, and a separately stipulated available fast perfect census that meets both. Do not choose a lower task/alert count without preserving the needed outcome. R8: Compare actual decision needs and prospective independent purposes. M03 illustrates why a larger history package need not be acquired when a smaller current discriminator answers this one question; no global ranking or automatic retirement follows. R9: One source may suffice for the actual claim while several consumers remain legitimate. A separately stricter actual question can justify another discriminator only under its own validity, dependence, cost and authority conditions. Existing sufficient traceability or direct evidence can avoid further work; meaningful latent purposes and categorical duties remain. R10: Compare the remaining acquisition, interpretation, coordination, action, verification and maintenance on the correct shared-resource horizon. Existing adequate state evidence can cost zero new acquisition; the direct state option can be cheaper than history for that one purpose, while a real learning duty can make history adequate and a state-only option inadequate. A diagram with communication coverage alone supplies no state evidence. Retain credible latent protection and categorical duties without inventing indefinite consumers.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p><a href="shared/a4.md#r-a4f28c9a5ea3980a77b8fd9dd516b196949af95ca6791b31859270e417736062">Shared scientific record a4f28c9a5ea3</a></p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><p><a L05 distinguishes a fast but risk-invalid n8 plan, a valid n9 plan that misses the joint window, and a separately stipulated available fast perfect census that meets both. this is not an all-pairs clique, compulsory workflow, empirical deployment or new authority.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Prior r6 current fields</dt><dd><dl><dt>Members</dt><dd><ol><li><p>P018</p></li><li><p>P020</p></li><li><p>P021</p></li><li><p>P023</p></li><li><p>P050</p></li><li><p>ECSE-14</p></li><li><p>ECSE-20</p></li><li><p>ECSE-21</p></li><li><p>ECSE-33</p></li><li><p>ESS-011</p></li><li><p>ESS-017</p></li><li><p>ESS-045</p></li><li><p>P003</p></li><li><p>P027</p></li><li><p>P061</p></li><li><p>P062</p></li><li><p>ESS-001</p></li><li><p>P026</p></li><li><p>ECSE-61</p></li><li><p>ESS-037</p></li><li><p>ESS-041</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Flow</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Define the complete achieved-state outcome, quality criterion, relevant deadline and actual dependencies.</p></li><li><p>Identify actual competing duties, actor availability, adjustment/resumption work and legitimate priority constraints.</p></li><li><p>State the conditional regime/time model and applicable event mixture; use a direct complete schedule or worst-case bound when sufficient.</p></li><li><p>Compare the joint demand/critical path with the actual required window, including intervention side effects and risk.</p></li><li><p>Choose an adequately discriminating monitor/adjustment policy or a legitimate direct/fixed/parallel/bounded-stop alternative; retain unresolved empirical and authority premises.</p></li><li><p><a href="shared/71.md#r-71bc8f9acba4ce5791ef624bc91cd907390d560e110a81b46bfb3ca6937ed9e7">Shared scientific record 71bc8f9acba4</a></p></li></ol></dd><dt>Joint cost</dt><dd><p>Acquisition, signal processing, task switching, manual intervention, resumption, whole recovery, concurrent required work, coordination, training/retention, maintenance and error/loss costs belong to the same horizon where shared. Do not double-count genuinely parallel steps or omit scarce resources.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p>Z04 is an invented finite schedule: recovery 4/12 plus another duty 1 and noise adjustment/resumption 6 against deadline 13. It establishes a conditional interaction, not an observed human distribution or operational control design. Native evidence and seven inherited author accesses retain their exact scopes. R6: B01 enumerates91 fixed plans and finds the shortest admissible complete path11 against deadline10; B02 shows inspection error can remove all admissible plans in that family. B03 changes actual resources and B04 changes the meaning of completed stable correction. These separate constructions do not strengthen Z04/H06 into an all-pairs-pass theorem. Adequate current/exhaustive evidence, no actual process claim, real parallel capacity and permitted bounded stop remain alternatives.</p></dd><dt>Alternatives</dt><dd><ol><li><p>No unwarranted adjustment under the stipulated routine-noise process; a justified experiment or urgent correction is a different case.</p></li><li><p>Exact current schedule or conservative bound rather than a population forecast.</p></li><li><p>Genuinely independent competent capacity, a feasible redesigned task or a legitimate bounded stop.</p></li><li><p>A legitimately different requirement or safe automated control when actually available; neither is assumed.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Rule refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>D09</p></li><li><p>D88</p></li><li><p>D103</p></li><li><p>D168</p></li><li><p>D169</p></li><li><p>D176</p></li><li><p>D177</p></li><li><p>D200</p></li><li><p>D201</p></li><li><p>D304</p></li><li><p>D305</p></li><li><p>D306</p></li><li><p>D307</p></li><li><p>D316</p></li><li><p>D317</p></li><li><p>D324</p></li><li><p>D325</p></li><li><p>D326</p></li><li><p>D327</p></li><li><p>D328</p></li><li><p>D329</p></li><li><p>D332</p></li><li><p>D333</p></li><li><p>D334</p></li><li><p>D335</p></li><li><p>D336</p></li><li><p>D337</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Case refs</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Z01</p></li><li><p>Z02</p></li><li><p>Z03</p></li><li><p>Z04</p></li><li><p>V05</p></li><li><p>V07</p></li><li><p>A10</p></li><li><p>A11</p></li><li><p>A16</p></li><li><p>B01</p></li><li><p>B02</p></li><li><p>B03</p></li><li><p>B04</p></li><li><p>B05</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Reentry</dt><dd><p>Changed event mixture, conditional durations, quality requirement, actor allocation, adjustment policy, shared resource or legitimate deadline reopens the affected configuration.</p></dd><dt>Review status</dt><dd><p>PENDING_CURRENT_FIDELITY_THEN_DISTINCT_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>R7 conditional member additions</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>R7 reassessment</dt><dd><p>Preserve the existing regime-specific complete recovery and bounded adjustment composition. Continuing lifecycle multiplicity is an additional constraint only when that consumer is actually present, and pending-work transfer matters only at an actual role transition. Neither is invented from group membership.</p></dd><dt>R7 remaining premises</dt><dd><p>The new L01 construction already fails for a pair of complete responses; it is not an all-pairs-pass/triple-fail witness. Do not double-count genuinely parallel steps or omit scarce resources. R7: Retain complete recovery, necessary resumption, risk-valid inspection/corrective work and actual joint duties without double counting a genuinely shared observation. Removing an unwarranted adjustment is legitimate; silently deleting a justified effect duty is not.</p></dd><dt>Evidence and limits</dt><dd><p>Z04 is an invented finite schedule: recovery 4/12 plus another duty 1 and noise adjustment/resumption 6 against deadline 13. It establishes a conditional interaction, not an observed human distribution or operational control design. Native evidence and seven inherited author accesses retain their exact scopes. R6: B01 enumerates91 fixed plans and finds the shortest admissible complete path11 against deadline10; B02 shows inspection error can remove all admissible plans in that family. B03 changes actual resources and B04 changes the meaning of completed stable correction. These separate constructions do not strengthen Z04/H06 into an all-pairs-pass theorem. Adequate current/exhaustive evidence, no actual process claim, real parallel capacity and permitted bounded stop remain alternatives. R7: The new L01 construction already fails for a pair of complete responses; it is not an all-pairs-pass/triple-fail witness. The original H06 regime/adjustment distinction and its narrower arity remain.
